| 2026-05-25 | Critical hardening review found Edit Child/Edit Class forms could reset typed values when background sync refreshed the underlying object reference. | Added one-time initialization guards to both edit screens and tests that rerender with refreshed objects while preserving in-progress edits. | Any edit form derives initial local state from context objects that can refresh while the user is typing. |
| 2026-05-25 | Critical hardening review found SQLite connection PRAGMAs were only guaranteed during migration runs. | Moved `foreign_keys`, `journal_mode`, and `busy_timeout` setup to the database client so every newly-opened Expo SQLite handle is configured, and reused the helper from migrations. | Any new database connection path is introduced or Expo SQLite handle lifecycle changes. |
| 2026-05-25 | Critical hardening review found missing regression guards for historic gender values, search-field text suggestions, global screen timers, and missing-user clock-in status. | Added tests for historic gender chips, search `Searchbar` no-suggestion props, recursive `src/screens` timer scanning, and null-user clock-in status. | Regression tests should scan the relevant surface instead of checking a hand-maintained shortlist. |
| 2026-05-25 | Critical hardening review found `AGENTS.md` lacked the temporary two-backend Supabase CLI warning and had stale support-export wording. | Restored the two-backend warning in `AGENTS.md` and updated support-export wording in both `AGENTS.md` and `CLAUDE.md` to current SQLite behavior. | Keep agent context files synchronized during cutover when old production and the new staging backend both exist. |
| 2026-05-25 | Critical hardening review noted non-blocking backlog items: injected git commit metadata, chip deselect, wider outbox batching, and redundant active time-entry reads. | Documented as deferred; they do not block cutover because release/backend identity is already visible, historic gender values are preserved, correctness does not depend on wider batching, and the redundant time-entry read is functional. | Revisit after cutover stabilization or if support diagnostics need exact build SHA provenance. |
| 2026-05-25 | Group creation and child-to-group adds failed sync with `new row violates row-level security policy` on `groups` and `child_group_memberships`. | `ChildrenContext.addGroup` / `addChildToGroup` now pass `created_by`; `groupsRepository.saveGroup` persists and enqueues `created_by`, creates a same-owner active `group_ea_assignments` row, and `addChildToGroup` derives membership owner plus `grouping_version_id` from the group. `syncAll()` repairs stale failed group and membership outbox payloads before retry so existing tester-device rows can heal. | Any new mobile-created table protected by RLS needs producer tests that prove the outbox payload includes the server-required owner/relationship columns. |
| 2026-05-25 | RLS/app contract audit found remaining structural mismatches after the group fix. | Wrote `documentation/archive/rls-app-contract-audit-2026-05-25.md`. Key blockers: `child_ea_assignments` has no live UPDATE policy despite app archive writes, and archive/unassignment flows need operation-aware outbox ordering so the app does not revoke the assignment needed by later relationship rows. High item: class edit/delete UI and creator-only class policies need a product decision. | Treat RLS as an open release gate until the blocker items are fixed or explicitly accepted. |
| 2026-05-26 | The RLS audit artifact was a point-in-time review, not a living table-by-table contract. | Added `documentation/rls-sync-contract-map.md` and updated `AGENTS.md` plus `documentation/DATABASE_SCHEMA_GUIDE.md` so future agents start from producer, operation, RLS, ordering, and tests together. Added an addendum to the 2026-05-25 audit noting that creator SELECT policies were intentionally restored after live-device upsert failures. | Any future audit must update the map, not only write a narrative report. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 6 review found support export diagnostic queries could fail silently and report empty sync state/outbox rows. | `debugDump()` now returns `{ error: String(error) }` for abnormal `sync_state` and `sync_outbox` query failures when the tables exist, while still returning empty arrays on a pre-migration fresh database. | Support tooling should distinguish "no stuck work" from "diagnostics could not be queried." |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 6 release-gate hardening initially put an integration-only runtime check in a `*.test.js` file, so default Jest ran it with the in-memory setup. | Renamed it to `test-support/integration/sqliteRuntime.integration.js` and included it only through `jest.integration.config.js`. | Integration-only checks should not match the default Jest discovery pattern. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 3 caller scan found existing generic `storage.setItem` and `STORAGE_KEYS` use in contexts, screens, and the AsyncStorage sanitizer. | Route those calls through the temporary SQLite-backed storage facade for now and save the grep output as a Plan 5 migration input. | Plan 5 starts context/screen migration; do not infer callsites from filenames. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 3 review found deterministic child relationship IDs erased handover/rejoin history. | Generate new UUIDs for new relationship rows and use active-row lookups to make repeated saves idempotent without overwriting ended rows. | Any relationship repository derives IDs from natural keys. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 3 review found `archiveChild` left active `child_group_memberships` rows. | Archive now ends active group memberships and enqueues archive outbox rows alongside EA, programme, and class relationships. | Any new child relationship table is added to archive and no-history checks together. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 3 review found `deleteClass` and `deleteGroup` were local-only hard deletes. | Both methods now route to archive, preserving rows locally and producing syncable archive outbox entries. | A future hard-delete-if-no-history product requirement is added. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 3 review found synced server rows could be re-enqueued by repository save helpers. | Added `shouldEnqueueOutbox` and skip outbox rows for `sync_status = 'synced'` or `terminal`; `syncStatusFromSynced(undefined)` now defaults to `pending` for local writes. | Plan 4 changes row status vocabulary or import paths for server pulls. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 TDD exposed that legacy session view-model fields could still reach Supabase from outbox payloads. | Added table-specific server-column allowlists and tests for sessions and assessments so `children_ids`, `group_ids`, `session_type_id`, `correct_letters`, and related local fields are stripped. | Any new repository enqueues a payload built from a screen-shaped object instead of normalized columns. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 TDD exposed that records using `local-legacy-programme` would be attempted against Supabase. | `offlineSync` now marks those rows terminal with a visible failed item instead of calling Supabase. | Plan 5 removes storage-facade fallback paths or active programme assignment is guaranteed before writes. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 review found `timeEntriesRepository` wrote domain rows without enqueueing outbox rows. | Added repository producer tests and same-transaction outbox enqueue for time-entry create/update, with synced server rows still suppressed. | Every repository write contract needs a producer-to-engine test, not only a repository-local outbox assertion. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 review found `in_flight` rows could be stranded forever after app restart. | Added restart-recovery coverage and `syncOutboxRepository.resetInFlight()`, called at the start of every `syncAll()` cycle. | Any status that excludes a row from readiness needs a restart recovery path. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 review found sync finalization could delete a newer local outbox payload created while the old payload was in flight. | Added a concurrent-edit test and compare-and-swap finalization; stale finalizers restore/leave the row pending and keep the domain row pending. | Multi-step state transitions need a stale-writer test that mutates storage while the mocked server call is in progress. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 review found null outbox payloads could be converted to `{ id }` and sent as empty upserts. | Non-delete records with missing payload now fail terminal locally with no Supabase call. | Outbox producers should treat payload absence as a data integrity bug, not as an implicit minimal payload. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 review agent found unknown-table rows could never terminalize after CAS finalization was added. | Added an outbox-only terminal finalizer and regression test so unknown tables become visible failed items without touching a domain table. | When finalization logic grows CAS predicates, malformed-row paths that never enter `in_flight` need separate coverage. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 5 review found `pullReferenceData` and `staffProgrammeAssignmentsRepository` had no production caller. | Authenticated startup now runs `pullReferenceData({ userId })` before publishing `user`, and the pull includes schools, job titles, programmes, academic years, assessment windows, teachers, and user-scoped staff programme assignments. | Tests that hand-insert producer rows as fixtures must also prove the real startup producer path exists. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 5 review found server-pull merges preserved every absent local row. | Children and classes context merges now drop synced absent rows while preserving pending, failed, and in-flight local work. | Any cache-first pull needs a test for both remote removal propagation and local dirty-row preservation. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 5 review found assessment and letter-tracker save handlers could reject without a user-visible recovery path. | Added controlled save errors and retry behavior for `LetterAssessmentScreen` and `LetterTrackerScreen`; failed writes do not navigate, refresh sync state, trigger background sync, or falsely update mastered-letter UI. | Every screen-owned async write handler needs a failure test, not only a success-path repository mock. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 5 review found several medium/low UI contracts were either missing or too large for the corrective slice. | Fixed user-scoped time stats, focus refreshes, stale lookup guards, and group membership sync-status refreshes; explicitly deferred active `child_class_memberships` as the source for class roster helpers and `class_grouping_state.class_list_status` UI gating to the next Plan 5 UX/data-flow slice. | Keep medium findings visible when they represent product/data-flow design, not a small bug patch. |
| 2026-05-22 | Android signed-in smoke found mobile-created classes were invisible after refresh because `class_ea_assignments` had no producer. | `classesRepository.saveClass` now creates and enqueues a `class_ea_assignments` row for the actor/programme in the same transaction; `ClassesContext.addClass` passes the active programme. | Any user-facing query filtered through a relationship table needs a matching producer test. |
| 2026-05-22 | Android signed-in smoke found `session_attendees` and `assessment_items` pushed composite local ids into UUID server columns. | Repositories now create deterministic UUID ids and `offlineSync` sanitizes older composite payloads before push. | Local id format must match server id type for every outbox-pushed table. |
| 2026-05-22 | Android signed-in smoke found session parent upsert failed RLS before attendees existed. | Added `20260522103000_masi_session_upsert_visibility.sql` with direct owner SELECT fallback and pushed it to `masi-app-sqlite`. | Upsert visibility policies need a direct-column fallback when dependent join rows sync later. |
| 2026-05-22 | Android kill/reopen smoke exposed startup `database is locked` from reference-data replacement bypassing the write queue. | `ReferenceDataRepository` now uses `runRepositoryTransaction`; regression coverage proves default reference replacements queue with other writes. | Any default repository write bypassing `runRepositoryTransaction` is a device-risk bug. |
| 2026-05-22 | Follow-up review found server-assigned children/classes would disappear offline because pulled junction joins were discarded. | `pullPreloadedChildData`, `ChildrenContext`, and `ClassesContext` now persist server-pulled child EA assignments, child programme enrollments, child class memberships, referenced classes, and class EA assignments as synced rows with no outbox. | For every INNER JOIN in a local read query, verify the joined table has a producer/hydration path. |
| 2026-05-22 | Follow-up review found `staff_programme_assignments.replaceFromServer` could not propagate server removals and could collide with the one-active-assignment index. | `replaceFromServer` now supports scoped replace for `staff_programme_assignments`, and `pullReferenceData` passes the authenticated user's scope. | Any user-scoped table with active-unique constraints needs scoped replacement or tombstone semantics. |
| 2026-05-22 | Follow-up review found assessment summary-item deterministic IDs could drift between repository creation and legacy payload sanitizing. | Added shared `assessmentItemDomainId` and `sessionAttendeeDomainId` helpers used by repositories and sync payload sanitizing. | Any deterministic id convention exists in more than one file. |
| 2026-05-22 | Follow-up review found successful server merges dropped terminal local rows. | Children and classes merge logic now preserves any absent local row whose sync status is not `synced`, including `terminal`. | Any new terminal status appears in domain rows. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 6 found Profile -> Export Database still serialized AsyncStorage as the primary database. | `exportDatabase()` now calls `debugDump()` and shares SQLite schema version, migrations, table counts, sync state, and failed/terminal outbox rows with app/build metadata. | Support exports should prove the actual active storage engine, not the old storage mechanism. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 6 found `src/utils/storage.js` still exposed generic `getItem`, `setItem`, `removeItem`, and `STORAGE_KEYS`. | Removed the generic public key/value facade, moved profile cache to SQLite `local_state`, and verified source AsyncStorage usage is limited to Supabase auth and logger paths. | Generic facades hide stale AsyncStorage assumptions; prefer typed repository or storage methods. |
| 2026-05-22 | Plan 6 found the repo had no explicit release-gate script or focused SQLite integration config. | Added `test:integration`, `test:release`, and `jest.integration.config.js`; the integration gate now runs in band under a separate file-backed better-sqlite setup so it can fail for SQLite/runtime issues the default suite may not surface. | Release gates should change at least one meaningful variable from the previous gate. |
| 2026-05-22 | Supabase advisors now report `auth_leaked_password_protection` on `masi-app-sqlite`. | This is a hosted Auth project setting, not an app-code or migration failure. Supabase docs place leaked-password protection in Auth password security and note it is available on Pro Plan and above. | Before external field cutover, decide whether to enable leaked-password protection through project settings or explicitly accept the warning for the current plan. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 4 review agent found user writes could inherit stale retry backoff metadata. | `insertOutboxRecord` now resets `retry_count`, `last_error`, and `next_retry_at` on enqueue conflict; time-entry update coverage proves fresh user edits retry immediately. | Any durable retry metadata colocated with the active outbox row must be cleared when the payload changes. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 auth review found profile fetches could complete after sign-out and repopulate stale state. | Added profile-load versioning and sign-out invalidation so stale local/server profile results are ignored. | Any context starts async work that can outlive the current authenticated user. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 context review found ChildrenContext still had separate mount pulls for children, groups, and memberships. | Added a one-call preloaded child data service and cache-preserving bundle hydration in ChildrenContext. | Any provider hydrates related domains through independent server calls. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 Task 4 found ChildrenContext and ClassesContext still used generic `storage.setItem`/`STORAGE_KEYS` list replacement for server pull cache writes. | Persist pulled rows through typed methods (`saveChild`, `saveGroup`, `saveChildrenGroup`, `saveClass`) so repository contracts remain the write boundary. | Any context merges server rows directly into local cache state. |
| 2026-05-21 | Clean-slate Plan 5 still had the legacy AsyncStorage schema sanitizer and bootstrap test. | Deleted `asyncStorageSanitizer`, `SchemaHardeningBootstrap`, and sanitizer state facade methods; fresh SQLite installs should rely on schema migrations and repository normalization instead. | User reintroduces in-place local data upgrades from legacy app builds. |
| 2026-05-21 | Android staging bundle failed after sanitizer deletion because `App.js` still imported `SchemaHardeningBootstrap`. | Removed the stale root import/wrapper and added an App-root smoke test that fails when deleted bootstrap imports remain. | Any cleanup deletes a provider/bootstrap component that is imported only from the root app entry. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 review found default reads could leak cross-programme sessions, assessments, mastery, groups, and classes. | Repository read APIs now accept `{ userId }`, resolve the actor's active programme, and user-facing screens/contexts pass the current user. No active programme returns an empty read set. | Any screen adds a repository read without passing `userId`; add a grep/review check. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 review found letter mastery reuse could mutate a row from another programme. | `persistLiteracySession`, `LetterTrackerScreen`, and `LetterTrackerBottomSheet` now scope mastery/assessment reads by active programme and child; regression tests prove cross-programme deleted mastery rows are not reused. | Any natural-key lookup ignores `programme_id`. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 review found child delete still used legacy `hidden_at` and class archive left active child class memberships. | `storage.deleteChild` now tries no-history hard delete, otherwise archives; class archive ends child-class memberships, nulls current child class pointers, and enqueues the child/member updates. | Any archive path changes relationship tables or display filters. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 review found removed group memberships could reappear in active lists. | `groupsRepository.getChildrenGroups()` excludes removed memberships by default and ChildrenContext filters removed memberships in group helpers and duplicate checks. | A history view needs removed memberships; call `includeRemoved: true` explicitly. |
| 2026-05-21 | Plan 5 review found Auth/Children/Classes context state could survive sign-out or stale async work. | Auth invalidates in-flight profile loads on manual sign-out; Children and Classes contexts clear state when user disappears and guard async state updates by active user id. | Any provider starts async hydration keyed to auth user id. |
| 2026-05-21 | Android Plan 5 validation surfaced repeated Supabase auth lock warnings during signed-in startup. | Aligned Supabase JS/Auth to 2.100.1, made the Supabase client a singleton, removed duplicate startup `getSession()`, and queued remaining startup reference-data reads. Fresh Android logcat stayed quiet for 35 seconds after restart. | Treat auth-lock warnings as device-validation findings, not harmless test noise, until verified on Android. |
| 2026-05-22 | Profile cache belongs in SQLite `local_state`, not AsyncStorage. | Plan 6 removed the last domain/profile AsyncStorage use from `src/utils/storage.js`; AsyncStorage remains only for Supabase auth session storage and app logs. | A third-party library requires profile cache to live in AsyncStorage. |
| 2026-05-22 | Profile -> Export Database exports SQLite diagnostics rather than raw AsyncStorage keys. | Field support needs schema version, table counts, sync state, and failed/terminal outbox rows; raw key-value dumps no longer represent the app database. Failed/terminal payloads can still include child/session/assessment PII, so the warning dialog remains mandatory. | A support workflow needs a separate redacted domain-row export, not just diagnostics. |
| 2026-05-22 | `test:release` is the automated Plan 6 branch gate. | It runs full Jest, focused file-backed SQLite integration coverage, and the SQLite staging environment guard before build/promotion decisions. | CI replaces local release gating with an equivalent checked workflow. |
| 2026-05-22 | Treat Plan 6 implementation as complete but not field-cutover complete. | Plans 1-6 have their code/doc/release-gate work in place and the core Android emulator outbox path passed, but final release still depends on physical-device validation and the user-owned cutover communication gate. | Physical device testing uncovers a release-blocking issue or the cutover plan changes. |
| 2026-05-25 | Pin the EAS `preview` APK profile to SQLite staging in `eas.json`. | A packaged app resolves `app.config.js` at EAS build time, and a missing target can silently select the hardcoded primary backend. Preview APKs must not depend on local `.env.local` or EAS-hosted env setup to avoid false validation against old production. | A separate production/staging release profile matrix replaces the current preview APK workflow. |
| 2026-05-25 | Close the SQLite cutover engineering gate, but keep field distribution gated. | Fresh automated release, integration, staging migration, dry-run, advisor, and whitespace checks passed with only documented Supabase advisor warnings. The remaining gates require user-run physical-device smoke and user-owned staff notification before distributing to field testers. | User physical-device testing finds a blocker, staff notification timing changes, or the target backend/build profile changes before distribution. |
| 2026-05-25 | Repair mobile-created group ownership before sync rather than weakening RLS. | Physical-device/simulator testing showed `groups` and `child_group_memberships` inserts failing RLS because local rows and outbox payloads missed `created_by`, and mobile-created groups also lacked a matching `group_ea_assignments` producer row. The correct fix is to make the mobile producer write the server-required ownership shape and heal stale failed outbox payloads before retry. | Product allows group creation without an active programme assignment, or group assignment creation moves to a server RPC. |
| 2026-07-14 | A child's reading level appeared to reset to “Not set” every session. | Added nullable current state on `children`, pre-fill, changed-only atomic child/outbox updates, server allowlisting, additive local/server migrations, and child-screen display/edit surfaces. Session-level snapshots remain intact for history. | Any future session form treats an event observation as current entity state without an explicit read-back source. |
| 2026-07-14 | Literacy form state could retain reading-level or tracker changes for a child removed from the final attendee list. | `persistLiteracySession` now treats `session.children_ids` as the authoritative aggregate boundary and filters both maps before session, child, or mastery writes. Real SQLite regressions prove non-attendee state cannot escape. | Any session extension adds another per-child map without filtering it through final attendees at the persistence boundary. |
| 2026-07-14 | Ordinary RLS-scoped pull success could under-return rows without an error and falsely authorize durable local relationship endings. | Built a server-authoritative acknowledgment RPC and changed both pull contexts so ordinary queries hydrate content but cannot prove absence. Missing, malformed, mismatched, or errored authority preserves local assignments, reports an operational failure, and withholds the successful-pull stamp. Live migration and authenticated probe remain required before operational closure. | Any new reconcile scope derives its absent ids from an ordinary filtered query instead of adding the relationship to the authoritative snapshot contract. |
| 2026-07-14 | One failed outbox row marked its whole table failed for same-pass dependency checks, blocking unrelated child subgraphs. | Replaced `failedTables` with exact failed/skipped record evidence, direct FK matching, and inverse archive subject matching. Matching dependents remain pending; unrelated records continue. | Any dependency code checks only a table name or a new archive edge lacks a subject-column mapping. |
| 2026-07-14 | A failed large batch could expand into as many as 1,000 concurrent per-record requests in one sync pass. | Separated scheduling, payload, and failure-work limits: 100 rows per batch, 25 individual fallback attempts per pass, and concurrency five. Unattempted rows remain pending and are reported as deferred, not failed. | Field telemetry shows the ceilings are too low for useful bad-row isolation or too high for weak devices/connections. |
| 2026-07-14 | Corrected sync code had no durable mechanism to revisit already-broken rows, while the historical group ownership heal still scanned every sync pass. | Added a monotonic startup repair ledger. Version 1 runs the idempotent group ownership cutover heal once; all sync entry points share and await the repair promise; failed recipes retry next launch without blocking the app. | A field fix needs to revisit a proven historical cohort; add a narrowly matched idempotent recipe and increment the repair version. |
| 2026-07-14 | SQLite bootstrap errors were caught inside asynchronous provider work, so navigation could mount against a database that had never opened or migrated. The only visible fallback depended on a synchronous React render error, and Export Database also required SQLite. | Added `DatabaseBootstrapGate` above every provider. It blocks entry, reports the failure, retries after the client disposes half-open connections, and exports AsyncStorage-backed logs. No destructive reset control is exposed. | Any new root provider performs local persistence work before the bootstrap gate, or log export gains a SQLite dependency. |
| 2026-07-21 | The Supabase CLI attempted to parse the repository `.env.local` before migration commands and rejected an unrelated key, even though the target-guarded helper had already parsed the needed SQLite values safely. | For this release, copied the small `supabase/` project directory to an isolated temporary directory and executed the existing guarded command plan there. The project ref, URL, and credentials still came from the validated helper; secrets stayed redacted. The migrations and live probe then passed. | Supabase CLI environment parsing changes, or the staging helper gains a tested isolated-workspace mode for remote CLI actions. |
| 2026-07-21 | The old tester loader could silently select the legacy project, printed a committed shared password, wrote retired profile columns, and omitted the active Programme assignment needed by onboarding. | Disabled `scripts/loadTestUsers.js` and hardened `scripts/createTesters.js` as an exact-project, zero-class-only pilot provisioner with per-user credentials, normalized profiles, active Programme assignments, idempotent reuse, conflict refusal, and dry-run support. | Any future staff-provisioning tool accepts generic Supabase environment names, prints credentials, or creates a profile without the grants the app requires. |

### 2026-06-17 — Tranche kickoff (Items 3–9)

- Confirmed Items 1–2 (write-storm / sync reliability) complete, device-verified by Jim, merged to `main`.

  tip `c183d3e`) and confirmed **all** portable assets the guide references exist
  (colours, capture spine, device-tier, contexts, full notification stack).

  Claude orchestrates + spec/quality reviews; Codex `/codex:adversarial-review` as the second
  reviewer; port the fork's field-tested design choices with Jim's sign-off; branch + PR per item.
- **Revised execution model (Jim, 2026-06-17):** build Items 3–9 **one item per session**,
  handing over to a fresh-context agent between items (via the `handoff` skill). This build-log
  + the Top-10 guide are the cross-session backbone, so context resets lose no knowledge.
  Confirmed: **keep Masi's blue brand — no orange.** The fork supplies token *architecture*, never *hue*.
- **Next:** Item 3 — design-token system (Masi-blue tint ramp derived from `#294A99`). Grounding the plan against fork + Masi `colors.js`.

### 2026-06-17 — Item 3: brand direction LOCKED (red-dominant, light Zazi canvas)

- **Decision (Jim):** red-dominant on a **light Zazi canvas**. Rationale: matches Masi's existing
  red-heavy web brand (solid red Donate CTA, red footer band, red section rules, red-tinted icon chips);
  blue read as generic/cold; red is a proven primary (Netflix/YouTube/Pinterest/Airbnb/Target). The
  field-tool canvas stays **light** (sunlight legibility) rather than the website's dark canvas; an
  optional **dark hero band** echoes the web drama.
- **Palette approach:** brand red derived from the existing `#E72D4D`; full 50–900 ramp; a
  **differentiated error red** (deeper/cooler + alert icon) to avoid brand-vs-error semantic collision;
  green stays semantic success (`#3FA535`) + success-surface trio; yellow minimised out of chrome;
  **no gradients** (solid fills); warm-light canvas + white cards + soft shadows; Zazi type hierarchy.
- **Next:** palette + mock Home screen rendered for Jim's visual sign-off → then write the Item 3 TDD
  plan → then orchestrate Codex builds with Claude + Codex dual review.

### 2026-06-17 — Item 3 Task 1: built, reviewed (Claude + Codex), fixes routed

- **Built (Codex):** `src/constants/colors.js` (red-dominant token system) + `__tests__/colors.test.js`
  (fail-closed pin). Commit `cb3ca8d4`. 9/9 green (independently re-run by the controller).
- **Reviewed — three passes converged (the two-LLM topology working):**
  - Controller (Claude) **+ Codex adversarial `[high]`** independently flagged the same gap: the fail-closed
    guard could NOT detect a *dropped* export key (it only iterated keys that exist) — the very import-safety
    property Task 1 exists to protect. → **Fix A:** static `REQUIRED_KEYS` enumeration vs `Object.keys(colors)`.

### 2026-06-18 — Item 4: discovery + plan written (decisions locked)

- **Plan:** [`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-18-item4-sequential-capture.md`](../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-18-item4-sequential-capture.md)
  — 13 TDD tasks (constants · scoring/record-builder · reducer · **capture_mode schema contract** · device-pref ·
  route resolver · **useAssessmentSession spine** · **LetterAssessmentScreen proving-slice refactor** · EgraLetterGrid
  readOnly/currentIndex · SequentialAssessmentScreen · nav register · entry-point routing · Profile toggle).
- **Discovery findings that shaped the plan** (verified across Masi + fork `c183d3e`):
  - **Save path is an impedance layer, so the hook stays schema-agnostic.** `assessmentsRepository.saveAssessment`
    takes the screen's "fat" record and itself injects `programme_id` (NOT-NULL, via `resolveProgrammeId`), maps
    `date_assessed→assessment_date` / `correct_responses→score` / `letters_attempted→total_items`, and splits the EGRA
    detail into the normalized `assessment_items` table (`__summary__` row + per-letter rows). The extracted hook just
    rebuilds the same fat object + `capture_mode`; **no repo-internals surgery.**
  - **`capture_mode` is a 3-file code contract**, not one: local migration (`src/db/migrations.js` v4) **+**
    `ASSESSMENT_COLUMNS` (`assessmentsRepository.js`) **+** `SERVER_COLUMNS` (`src/services/offlineSync.js:195`) — miss
    the last and it persists locally but never syncs — **+** Supabase migration **+** contract map **+** refactor log.
  - **Column is nullable, NO DB default.** `NULL = legacy/grid`; a `DEFAULT 'sequential'` would mislabel grid rows
    written by older field app versions → corrupts the A/B. Client stamps the resolved mode explicitly.
  - **`correction_count`** rides in the existing `__summary__` metadata JSON (one line in `buildSummary`) → **no extra
    migration.**
  - **Three** assessment entry points exist (not two): `AssessmentChildSelectScreen:73`, `ChildAssessmentSummaryScreen:117`
    ("Run Assessment"), `AssessmentResultsScreen:34` ("Try Again").
  - `EgraLetterGrid` lacks `readOnly`/`currentIndex` (only `disabled`, which dims) → Task 9 adds them backward-compatibly.
  - CONTEXT.md already defines **"marking mode"** (who scores) → plan adds a **"capture mode"** glossary entry to prevent
    drift (orthogonal axis: which UI mechanic).
- **Decisions locked (Jim, via question):**
  1. **Preference scope = device-local + seam** (mirror the field-tested fork; `resolveCaptureMode` carries org/user as
     no-op seams). No new write path to the read-only `users` table. Default = `sequential`.
  2. **"Try Again" routing deferred to Item 5.** Route only the two clean entry points now;
     `AssessmentResultsScreen.handleTryAgain` keeps launching the grid until Item 5 (which owns that screen + has
     unrelated uncommitted edits there) routes it. **Disclosed gap, not silently dropped.**
- **Branch:** `feature/sequential-capture` off `main` (per handoff).
- **Plan reviewed (2026-06-18, two-LLM):** Claude `plan-reviewer` (SHIP-WITH-FIXES) + Codex adversarial
  (NEEDS-REWORK) — **converged**; core design confirmed sound (sync contract, nullable/no-default, record
  builder, correction_count round-trip, hook failed-save guard), and **8 fixes folded into the plan's
  "Post-review revisions" section**: sequential reducer **race clamp** (fixes a latent bug in the *fork* —
  reducer-level, not just the component guard), grid **finish-freeze** (`finishStartedRef` + `setPhase('finished')`
  before the Last-Attempted sheet) + grid **correction tracking** (un-taps → symmetric A/B data, not 0),
  existing-test reconcile (`LetterAssessmentScreen.plan5.test.js`), `LOCAL_STATE_KEYS` buildability fix (Masi
  uses direct keys), async double-launch `launchingRef` guard, non-tautological elapsed-via-expiry test,
  push-allowlist (`SERVER_COLUMNS`) test. **Next:** dispatch Codex Task 1.

### 2026-06-18 — Item 4: field-feedback follow-ups (Jim's first device pass)

- **Supabase migration APPLIED** (`b43b3c1`): `npm run sqlite:staging:push` → `capture_mode` on `masi-app-sqlite`
  (+ caught up 2 prior pending forward-prep migrations); dry-run confirms "up to date". Deploy gate cleared.
- **UX (`4215c4f`):** Step-by-Step now fills an explicitly-marked **Incorrect** item red (`colors.error` + white
  text + 'incorrect' a11y label) instead of leaving it blank — `EgraLetterGrid` `letterStates===false` (grid mode
  never stores false, so it's unaffected). Sequential **Back** button → neutral ink (frequent benign action) vs
  **End Assessment** red (rare consequential) so they no longer blur. **Owed:** Jim's visual confirm on device.
- **Sync bug B (`d2de9fc`):** Sync Status no longer says "Everything is up to date" while Failed Items exist.
  Root: terminal outbox rows are excluded from the `breakdown`/unsynced count but shown in Failed Items; now
  "up to date" requires no pending AND no failed, else a "<N> failed to sync — see below" message. UI-only.
- **Sync bug C (`7afa3da`, Jim-approved C1):** **Sync Now (force) now resurrects terminal rows** so it clears stuck
  dependency chains in one tap; auto-sync still skips terminal (no storms). Root: RLS/FK errors (42501/23503) are
  classified terminal and `getReadyRecords` only claimed pending+failed — so only per-item Retry cleared them, even
  though these are usually TRANSIENT (a child's parent had not synced yet). Fix: `getReadyRecords` gains
  `includeTerminal`; `syncAll` passes `includeTerminal: force`. **Owed: Jim's device re-test** (real two-connection
  behavior isn't unit-testable). The stuck records Jim saw were a one-time artifact of capturing *before* the
  migration (parent assessments `PGRST204`'d → child `assessment_items` `42501`'d as terminal); the migration +
  retry resolved them.
- **Test-infra (`6d4a8d3`, pre-existing — NOT a feature regression):** the full Jest suite surfaced a
  `captureModeMigration` failure that only appears in the full run order (passes in isolation + every serial subset).
  **Root, proven by a schema-DDL assertion:** the `capture_mode` CHECK is correctly in the migrated schema; some
  earlier test file leaves CHECK *enforcement* disabled process-wide on a later fresh better-sqlite3 `:memory:` db.
  Made the assertion schema-based (deterministic) → **full suite green again: 107 suites / 606 tests.** Real
  enforcement is covered server-side (Supabase named CHECK) + app-level (`isValidCaptureMode`). **Follow-up
  (separate, low priority):** find the file that disables CHECK enforcement and restore isolation.

  - **Gap 1 is half-done.** `syncContractCompleteness.test.js` already pins `SERVER_COLUMNS` vs the LOCAL migrated schema; the missing, field-critical half is the SERVER mirror (parse `supabase/migrations/*.sql`, assert each `SERVER_COLUMNS` column exists server-side — the PGRST204 direction). Task 1 is that complement, not a new suite.
  - **Gap 2 is 4 screens, not 5** — `SyncStatusScreen` already has `syncStatusScreen.test.js` (§8's list was stale).
  - **Gap 3:** `timeEntriesRepository` confirmed absent from `jest.integration.config.js` testMatch; the repo test FILE already exists (7 tests) but runs only in the unit tier — gap = wire it into the integration tier + add the provider-backed vertical.
  - **Gap 4 splits CI-safe from live:** guard + policy-targeting unit test in CI; the live RLS probe is opt-in (Jim runs interactively — mgmt token 401s in agent shells), owed like Item 4's device pass.
  - **Gap 5:** the fork's `expoSQLiteRealEngine.js` (165 lines; per-name file registry + close/reopen survival) is portable, added **OPT-IN** (does not replace `expoSQLiteMock.js` or rewire the 20 integration suites).
- **Plan reviewed (2026-06-18, two-LLM):** Claude `plan-reviewer` (**SHIP-WITH-FIXES**, 5 findings) + Codex adversarial (**NEEDS-REWORK**, 5 findings) — **convergent → reconciled SHIP-WITH-FIXES** (both endorsed the gap mapping + risk boundaries). Review file: `docs/plan-reviews/2026-06-18-item8-field-critical-paths-plan-review.md`. **7 fixes folded as R1–R7** in the plan's "Post-review revisions" section (the 3 HIGH ones controller-re-verified against source):
  - **R1 (HIGH, both)** Task 1 parser must capture multi-column `ALTER…ADD COLUMN, ADD COLUMN` + treat `do $$` constraint blocks as opaque (`20260521144901_masi_zazi_alignment_schema.sql:127-181` adds ~18 `SERVER_COLUMNS` columns via comma-separated ALTERs — a first-column-only parser is the load-bearing false-green).
  - **R2 (HIGH, plan-reviewer)** Task 6 WIRES the EXISTING `timeEntriesRepository.test.js` (7 tests) into the integration tier — does NOT recreate it (literal "create" would overwrite stronger coverage).
  - **R3 (HIGH, Codex)** Task 8 ported engine must honor `useNewConnection` (distinct handle, same file) so the reader's `PRAGMA query_only=ON` can't poison the writer (`client.js:13-16,56-62`).
  - **R4 (HIGH/MED, both)** Task 7 precedent = `clientWriterConnection.test.js` (`__setDatabaseFactory` + `resetDatabaseConnectionForTests`) with a shared REAL db, not `ChildrenContext.test.js`.
  - **R5 (MED, both)** Task 3 add the `useChildren` mock (`ChildSelector` throws without it) · **R6 (MED)** Task 9 real policy = `sessions_select_own_or_assigned_child_history` · **R7 (LOW)** Task 5 `childrenGroups` is an array.
- **Orchestration (unchanged from Item 4):** Codex builds TDD via `codex:codex-rescue`; controller independently re-runs every focused suite + scope-checks every diff + commits; Tasks 1, 7, 8 (and 2) get a Codex adversarial pass. Testing-item red/green: characterization tests go green on first run (a red = wiring bug or a real defect → `systematic-debugging`); the gap-1 guard is proven-to-bite via a mutation check. **Leave-untouched list carried** (`AssessmentResultsScreen` WIP etc.). **Next:** dispatch Codex Task 1.

Risky tasks were adversarially verified by the controller against source (mutation-bite, premise-grep, full-tier no-regression) rather than a separate Codex round once the parser/premise/engine were reviewed.
- **Commits (one per task, off the plan commit `99db564`):** T1 `5e6e041` (SERVER_COLUMNS server-schema PGRST204 guard) · T2 `e99b92c` (HomeScreen render) · T3 `5a2f288` (LiteracySessionForm render) · T4 `8672e72` (TimeTrackingScreen render) · T5 `b844720` (ClassDetailScreen render) · T6 `fce8d7b` (timeEntriesRepository → integration tier + clock-out assertion) · T7 `42b15b0` (clock-in vertical through real SQLite) · T8 `e64a0a1` (device-faithful engine port + force-stop test) · T9 `9367000` (opt-in RLS visibility probe + CI guard).

The adversarial DDL sweep surfaced the one generated column (`display_name`) and confirmed the parser captures it.

Engine is OPT-IN (`expoSQLiteMock.js` unchanged; only the new test uses it). Full integration tier re-run confirmed **no regression** to the existing 20 suites.
  - **R4** (Task 7): bound the singleton `timeEntriesRepository` to a shared real `better-sqlite3` db via `__setDatabaseFactory` + `resetDatabaseConnectionForTests` (the `clientWriterConnection.test.js` precedent, not `ChildrenContext.test.js`). **Premise verified:** the repository is NOT mocked — `handleSignIn()` writes through the real path; the row + `insert` outbox land in the test db.

### 2026-06-19 — Item 8 follow-up: RLS probe env fix (made the owed live probe runnable)

- **The owed `npm run rls:probe` failed on Jim's first real run** — two bugs the CI-safe test couldn't catch because it fed `validateProbeEnv` synthetic objects and never exercised the real env wiring (the classic "the live run exists to catch this"): (1) the probe read `process.env` directly and **never loaded `.env.local`**; (2) Codex invented var names (`SUPABASE_URL_SQLITE` / `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY_SQLITE` / `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY_SQLITE`) that don't match the project's `*_SQLITE` convention.
- **Fix (`fix/rls-probe-env`, merged to local `main`):** the probe now auto-loads `.env`/`.env.local` via `sqlite-staging.cjs`'s exported `parseEnvContent`, and uses the real names — `SUPABASE_PROJECT_URL_SQLITE`, `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY_SQLITE` (anon/EA client), `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY_SQLITE` (service-role/secret, admin seed), `SUPABASE_PROJECT_ID_SQLITE`. CI-safe test updated to the new names (4/4 green). Verified: the probe now loads `.env.local` and fails only on the missing secret key.
- **Was owed (Jim):** add `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY_SQLITE` (the masi-app-sqlite **service-role/secret** key — Supabase dashboard → the sqlite project → Settings → API) to `.env.local`, then `npm run rls:probe`.
- **✓ CLEARED — live run passed (Jim, 2026-06-19):** `npm run rls:probe` → **all four rules PASS** live (`children`/`classes`/`groups` `*_select_created_by` + `sessions_select_own_or_assigned_child_history`). The full behavioral path (create test EA → seed → sign in as EA → upsert per rule → assert SELECT-visibility) succeeded, so the documented upsert-visibility contract is now **behaviorally verified** against masi-app-sqlite, not just asserted. **Item 8 has no remaining owed items.** The probe is reusable as a pre-ship guard for any future RLS-policy change (`npm run rls:probe`).

### 2026-06-19 — Item 5: kickoff — brainstorm, the group-centric split, spec + plan + two-LLM review

- **Item 8 merged to local `main`** (the RLS-probe-passed tip `8c48b8d`); branched `feature/child-results` off it. The handoff said base `21e1e65`, but `main` had advanced to `8c48b8d` (the Item-8 RLS docs commit) — branched off the current tip.
- **Brainstorm outcome — Item 5 was SPLIT.** §5 ("Child Results workflow") originally bundled: extract a mastery panel, embed in a renamed results screen, flip the ClassDetail row tap, restructure nav, fold last-session letters into the session form. Mid-brainstorm Jim raised moving to a **group-centric** UX (group cards w/ last/next summaries, group-first capture — the Zazi pattern). Grounding against `CONTEXT.md` confirmed group-centric is a **documented, settled** direction (`:37` all programmes group-based; `:100-101` child-picking is "a gap we want to close"; `:60` one session = one group-block; `sessions.group_id` exists). **Decision (Jim): ship the group-agnostic per-child foundation now (A–E); make group-centric its own next item.** §5 sub-items F (row-tap flip) and G (session fold-in) are **superseded by group cards** and deferred to that item — not dropped.
- **NEW domain requirement captured for the group item (Jim):** EAs **deliberately need whole-class access** — for (1) control-group assessment (assess the *entire* class though they only deliver to ~12: assessment scope ⊋ delivery scope) and (2) replacement (swap a churned child from the class). This **supersedes `CONTEXT.md:149`** (EA sees only assigned children); the group item must reconcile it via a scoped RLS/sync expansion + `grill-with-docs` + an ADR. Recorded in the spec §7 + persistent memory.
- **Spec:** [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-19-item5-child-results-design.md`](../docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-19-item5-child-results-design.md) (`e7963ed`). **Plan:** [`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-19-item5-child-results.md`](../docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-19-item5-child-results.md) (`355d617`) — 5 TDD task slices grounded in two live-code Explore passes (verified §5's stale line numbers; the panel must self-measure width via `onLayout`, and `components/assessment/` is singular in Masi).
- **Plan reviewed (two-LLM, 2026-06-19):** Codex adversarial + a Claude reviewer — **converged, structurally clean** (both re-verified line-ranges, caller/nav analysis). Findings folded as **R1–R7** (`018dfd2`), each verified against source: R2 **build-breaker** (removed an unreachable language-fallback test+guard — `normalizeLanguageKey` always maps to a supported key); R1 mock-accumulation `beforeEach`; R4 reactivation test; R3/R5/R6/R7 notes. The Codex run stalled once (600s watchdog) and the `plan-reviewer` agent dead-locked on a wait-for-codex loop → re-ran Codex and used `general-purpose` for the Claude lens.

- **Build method:** subagent-driven — Codex (`codex:codex-rescue`) built each task TDD red→green (build+test only); the controller independently re-ran every focused suite (Node 20), scope-checked every diff, ran adversarial reviews on the HIGH-risk tasks, and committed (Codex sandbox blocks `.git`).

Caught a **process bug**: the plan's Step-5 commit staged only `AppNavigator.js` — staged `ClassDetailScreen.js` (comment fix) too.
  - **Final whole-branch review (opus): READY TO MERGE.** Spec A–E delivered end-to-end, cross-task consistent (zero `ChildAssessmentSummary` refs), deferred F/G honored (ClassDetail row tap still → EditChild; LiteracySessionForm untouched). **Corrected a T5 LOW:** the Children tab `title` is NOT dead — it's the bottom-tab label (no `tabBarLabel` override); keep it.
- **Full suite (Node 20): 117 unit suites / 636 tests GREEN** (baseline 115/629 + Item-5 additions: `LetterMasteryPanel` + `ChildResults` suites; AssessmentResults +2 routing). Integration tier untouched.

(`npm run sqlite:staging:ios` or EAS `--profile preview`.)
